Appendix:

Appendix A
Kinematics of Four-Point KK Scattering Amplitudes

In this Appendix we present the kinematics of four-particle scattering processes of the KK states in the (4+1)d spacetime. The Minkowski metric tensor is chosen as

\eta^{MN}\!\!=\!\eta_{MN}\!\!=\!\text{diag}(-1,1,1,1,1)\hskip 0.85358pt
.

A.1
Kinematics for Massless Scattering Amplitudes
in 5d Spacetime

The 5d momenta in the center-of-mass (CM) frame are defined
as follows:

\displaystyle p_{1}^{M}

\displaystyle=-E_{1}\left(1,0,0,1,0\right)\hskip-0.85358pt,

\displaystyle p_{2}^{M}=-E_{2}\left(1,0,0,-1,0\right)\hskip-0.85358pt,

(192)

\displaystyle p_{3}^{M}

\displaystyle=E_{3}\left(1,s_{\theta},0,c_{\theta},0\right)\hskip-0.85358pt,

\displaystyle p_{4}^{M}=E_{4}\left(1,-s_{\theta},0,-c_{\theta},0\right)\hskip-0.85358pt,

In the above and hereafter, all the external momenta of each
scattering process are defined as out-going. The 5d Mandelstam variables are defined in terms of the momenta (
192
):

\displaystyle\hat{s}=-(\hat{p}_{1}\!+\hat{p}_{2})^{2},\hskip 10.00002pt\hat{t}=-(\hat{p}_{1}\!+\hat{p}_{4})^{2},\hskip 10.00002pt\hat{u}=-(\hat{p}_{1}\!+\hat{p}_{3})^{2}.

(193)

We can decompose each 5d momentum of Eq.(
192
)
into a 4d component plus a vanishing 5d component:

\hat{p}_{j}^{M}=\hskip 0.85358pt\big(p_{j}^{\mu},\,0\big),\hskip 10.00002pt~~~(j=1,2,3,4)\hskip 0.85358pt.

(194)

Accordingly, the 4d Mandelstam variables are defined as usual:

\displaystyle s=-(p_{1}\!+p_{2})^{2},\hskip 10.00002ptt=-(p_{1}\!+p_{4})^{2},\hskip 10.00002ptu=-(p_{1}\!+p_{3})^{2},

(195)

which equal the 5d Mandelstam variables
(\hat{s},\hat{t},\hat{u})
. Then, we define the transverse polarization vectors for each
massless gauge boson in 5d as follows:

\displaystyle\hat{\zeta}_{1+}^{M}\!=\hat{\zeta}_{2-}^{M}\!

\displaystyle=\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{\sqrt{2\,}\,}$}(0,1,\text{i},0,0)\hskip 0.85358pt,\hskip 20.00003pt

\displaystyle\hat{\zeta}_{3+}^{M}\!=\hat{\zeta}_{4-}^{M}\!=\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{\sqrt{2\,}\,}$}(0,-\text{i}c_{\theta},1,\text{i}s_{\theta},0)\hskip 0.85358pt,

\displaystyle\hat{\zeta}_{1-}^{M}\!=\hat{\zeta}_{2+}^{M}\!

\displaystyle=\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{\sqrt{2\,}\,}$}(0,1,-\text{i},0,0)\hskip 0.85358pt,\hskip 20.00003pt

\displaystyle\hat{\zeta}_{3-}^{M}\!=\hat{\zeta}_{4+}^{M}\!=\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{\sqrt{2\,}\,}$}(0,\text{i}c_{\theta},1,-\text{i}s_{\theta},0)\hskip 0.85358pt,

(196)

\displaystyle\hat{\zeta}_{10}^{M}\!=\hat{\zeta}_{20}^{M}\!

\displaystyle=(0,0,0,0,1)\hskip 0.85358pt,\hskip 10.00002pt

\displaystyle\hat{\zeta}_{30}^{M}\!=\hat{\zeta}_{40}^{M}\!=(0,0,0,0,1)\hskip 0.85358pt.

A.2
Kinematics for KK Scattering Amplitudes
under 5d Compactification

With the compactified 5d space, the momenta of a given four-particle
scattering process in the center-of-mass frame are defined as follows:

\displaystyle\hat{p}_{1}^{\prime M}

\displaystyle=-(E_{1},0,0,q,-M_{\mathfrak{n}_{1}}\hskip-0.85358pt)\hskip 0.85358pt,

\displaystyle\hat{p}_{2}^{\prime M}=-(E_{2},0,0,-q,-M_{\mathfrak{n}_{2}}\hskip-0.85358pt)\hskip 0.85358pt,

(197)

\displaystyle\hat{p}_{3}^{\prime M}

\displaystyle=(E_{3},q^{\prime}s_{\theta},0,q^{\prime}\hskip-0.85358ptc_{\theta},M_{\mathfrak{n}_{3}}\hskip-0.85358pt)\hskip 0.85358pt,

\displaystyle\hat{p}_{4}^{\prime M}=(E_{4},-q^{\prime}s_{\theta},0,-q^{\prime}\hskip-0.85358ptc_{\theta},M_{\mathfrak{n}_{4}}\hskip-0.85358pt)\hskip 0.85358pt,

where the fifth component of each 5d momentum is discretized as the KK mass-parameter
M_{\mathfrak{n}_{i}}\!\!=\!\mathfrak{n}_{i}/\hskip-0.85358ptR
. The 5d momentum conservation leads to the KK mass conservation

M_{\mathfrak{n}_{1}}\!\!+\!M_{\mathfrak{n}_{2}}\!\!+\!M_{\mathfrak{n}_{3}}\!\!+\!M_{\mathfrak{n}_{4}}\!\!=\hskip-0.85358pt0\hskip 0.85358pt

and the 4d energy conservation

E_{1}\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358ptE_{2}\hskip-0.85358pt=\hskip-0.85358ptE_{3}\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358ptE_{4}\hskip-0.85358pt=\!\hskip-0.85358pt\sqrt{s\,}\hskip 0.85358pt
. The 4d energy conservation determines the sizes of
the spatial momenta
q
and
q^{\prime}
as follows:

\displaystyle q

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\,2\sqrt{s\,}\,}\!\left([s-(M_{\mathfrak{n}_{1}}\!\!+\!M_{\mathfrak{n}_{2}})^{2}][s-(M_{\mathfrak{n}_{1}}\!\!-\!M_{\mathfrak{n}_{2}})^{2}]\right)^{\!\frac{1}{2}},

(198)

\displaystyle q^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\,2\sqrt{s\,}\,}\!\left([s-(M_{\mathfrak{n}_{3}}\!\!+\!M_{\mathfrak{n}_{4}})^{2}][s-(M_{\mathfrak{n}_{3}}\!\!-\!M_{\mathfrak{n}_{4}})^{2}]\right)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}.

Each 5d momentum in Eq.(
197
) can be decomposed
into a 4d momentum and a discretized mass-component in the
compactified fifth dimension:

p_{j}^{\hskip 0.85358pt\prime M}=(\hskip 0.85358ptp_{j}^{\hskip 0.85358pt\prime\mu},\,M_{\mathfrak{n}_{j}})\hskip 0.85358pt,\hskip 10.00002pt~~~(j=1,2,3,4)\hskip 0.85358pt.

(199)

Using Eqs.(
193
),(
195
)
and Eqs.(
197
),(
199
),
we can connect the Mandelstam variables in 4d and 5d as follows:

\displaystyle\hat{s}=s-(M_{\mathfrak{n}_{1}}\!\!+\hskip-0.85358ptM_{\mathfrak{n}_{2}})^{2},\hskip 20.00003pt\hat{t}=t-(M_{\mathfrak{n}_{1}}\!\!+\hskip-0.85358ptM_{\mathfrak{n}_{4}})^{2},\hskip 20.00003pt\hat{u}=u-(M_{\mathfrak{n}_{1}}\!\!+\hskip-0.85358ptM_{\mathfrak{n}_{3}})^{2},

(200)

where the sum of each set of Mandelstam variables obeys

\hat{s}+\hat{t}+\hat{u}=0\hskip 0.85358pt,\hskip 17.07164pts+t+u=\sum_{j=1}^{4}\!M_{\mathfrak{n}_{j}}^{2}\hskip 0.85358pt.\

(201)

The transverse polarization verctors for the external KK gauge bosons
take the same forms as those defined in Eq.(
A.1
),
whereas the longitudinal polarization vectors are defined as follows:

\displaystyle\hat{\zeta}_{1L}^{M}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\,M_{\mathfrak{n}_{1}}}(q,0,0,E_{1},0)\hskip 0.85358pt,\hskip 20.00003pt

\displaystyle\hat{\zeta}_{2L}^{M}=\frac{1}{\,M_{\mathfrak{n}_{2}}}(q,0,0,-E_{2},0)\hskip 0.85358pt,

(202)

\displaystyle\hat{\zeta}_{3L}^{M}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\,M_{\mathfrak{n}_{3}}}(q^{\prime},E_{3}s_{\theta},0,E_{3}c_{\theta},0)\hskip 0.85358pt,\hskip 20.00003pt

\displaystyle\hat{\zeta}_{4L}^{M}=\frac{1}{\,M_{\mathfrak{n}_{4}}}(q^{\prime},-E_{4}s_{\theta},0,-E_{4}c_{\theta},0)\hskip 0.85358pt.

Appendix B
Kinematic Numerators for KK Gauge Boson Scattering Amplitudes

In this Appendix, we present systematically the
kinematic numerators of the various four-point
scattering amplitudes of the massive KK gauge bosons at tree level
under the 5d toroidal compactification of
S^{1}
without orbifold. (Some of the four-point KK gauge boson amplitudes under the 5d
orbifold compactification of
S^{1}\!/\mathbb{Z}_{2}
were computed
previously
Hang:2021fmp
5DYM2002
5DSM2003
.)

We first summarize the
the kinematic numerators for the
four-point scattering amplitudes
of 5d massless gauge bosons as follows:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{N}}_{s}\,

\displaystyle=\,-\Big[(\hat{p}_{1}\!-\hat{p}_{2})(\hat{\zeta}_{1}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{2})+2(\hat{p}_{2}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{1})\hat{\zeta}_{2}-2(\hat{p}_{1}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{2})\hat{\zeta}_{1}\Big]\!\cdot\!\Big[(\hat{p}_{4}\!-\hat{p}_{3})(\hat{\zeta}_{3}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{4})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+2(\hat{p}_{3}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{4})\hat{\zeta}_{3}-2(\hat{p}_{4}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{3})\hat{\zeta}_{4}\Big]-\hat{s}\hskip 0.85358pt\Big[(\hat{\zeta}_{1}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{3})(\hat{\zeta}_{2}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{4})\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt(\hat{\zeta}_{1}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{4})(\hat{\zeta}_{2}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{3})\Big],

(203a)

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{N}}_{t}\,

\displaystyle=\,\Big[(\hat{p}_{1}\!-\hat{p}_{4})(\hat{\zeta}_{1}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{4})+2(\hat{p}_{4}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{1})\hat{\zeta}_{4}-2(\hat{p}_{1}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{4})\hat{\zeta}_{1}\Big]\cdot\Big[(\hat{p}_{2}\!-\hat{p}_{3})(\hat{\zeta}_{2}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{3})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+2(\hat{p}_{3}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{2})\hat{\zeta}_{3}-2(\hat{p}_{2}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{3})\hat{\zeta}_{2}\Big]+\hat{t}\hskip 0.85358pt\Big[(\hat{\zeta}_{1}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{3})(\hat{\zeta}_{2}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{4})+(\hat{\zeta}_{1}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{2})(\hat{\zeta}_{3}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{4})\Big],

(203b)

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{N}}_{u}\,

\displaystyle=\,-\Big[(\hat{p}_{1}\!-\hat{p}_{3})(\hat{\zeta}_{1}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{3})+2(\hat{p}_{3}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{1})\hat{\zeta}_{3}-2(\hat{p}_{1}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{3})\hat{\zeta}_{1}\Big]\cdot\Big[(\hat{p}_{2}\!-\hat{p}_{4})(\hat{\zeta}_{2}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{4})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+2(\hat{p}_{4}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{2})\hat{\zeta}_{4}-2(\hat{p}_{2}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{4})\hat{\zeta}_{2}\Big]+\hat{u}\hskip 0.85358pt\Big[(\hat{\zeta}_{1}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{4})(\hat{\zeta}_{2}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{3})-(\hat{\zeta}_{1}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{2})(\hat{\zeta}_{3}\!\cdot\hat{\zeta}_{4})\Big].

(203c)

\blacklozenge
Inelastic KK Scattering Amplitudes of

\,\{0,0,n,n
}:

For the inelastic scattering channel
\{0,0,n,n\}
,
there are two combinations of KK indices,

\mathsf{A}\!=\!\{0,0,\pm n,\mp n\}
,
which correspond to the same scattering amplitude. We derive the numerators of the KK scattering amplitude

\mathcal{T}\big[\hskip-0.85358ptA^{a\hskip 0.85358pt0}_{\pm 1}A^{b\hskip 0.85358pt0}_{\mp 1}A^{c\hskip 0.85358pt\pm n}_{L}\hskip-1.42262ptA^{d\hskip 0.85358pt\mp n}_{L}\big]\hskip 0.85358pt

as follows:

\mathcal{N}_{s}^{\mathsf{A}}\!=0\,,\hskip 20.00003pt\mathcal{N}_{t}^{\mathsf{A}}\!=-\,\mathcal{N}_{u}^{\mathsf{A}}=\frac{~M_{n}^{2}~}{4}[\hskip 0.85358pt(\bar{s}+4)\!-\hskip-0.85358pt(\bar{s}+4)c_{2\theta}\hskip 0.85358pt]\hskip 0.85358pt,

(204)

where
\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}=s/M_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.85358pt
. Under high energy expansion, we derive the following
leading-order (LO) kinematic numerators:

\mathcal{N}_{t}^{0,\mathsf{A}}=-\mathcal{N}_{u}^{0,\mathsf{A}}=\frac{~\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358ptM_{n}^{2}~}{4}(1\!-\hskip-0.85358ptc_{2\theta})\hskip 0.85358pt.

(205)

These LO numerators will be needed for computing the LO
scattering amplitudes of KK gauge bosons and of KK gravitons
(via double-copy) and for verifying the identity
(
81
).

\blacklozenge
Inelastic KK Scattering Amplitudes of

\,\{n,2n,3n,4n
}:

For the inelastic scattering channel
\{n,2n,3n,4n\}
,
there are two combinations of KK indices,

\mathsf{A}\!=\!\{\pm n,\mp 2n,\mp 3n,\pm 4n\}
,
which correspond to the same scattering amplitude. We compute the numerators of the KK scattering amplitude

\mathcal{T}\big[\hskip-0.85358ptA^{a\hskip 0.85358pt\pm n}_{L}A^{b\hskip 0.85358pt\mp 2n}_{L}A^{c\hskip 0.85358pt\mp 3n}_{L}\hskip-1.42262ptA^{d\hskip 0.85358pt\pm 4n}_{L}\big]

as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s}^{\mathsf{A}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{~M_{n}^{2}(49\bar{s}^{\prime}\!-\!1)~}{672\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{\prime}}[(7203\bar{s}^{\prime 2}\!-\!4410\bar{s}^{\prime}\!+\!375)+\bar{\omega}_{-}\hskip 0.85358pt(1519\bar{s}^{\prime}\!+\!125)c_{\theta}\hskip 0.85358pt]\,,

(206a)

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{t}^{\mathsf{A}}

\displaystyle=\frac{~M_{n}^{2}(49\bar{s}^{\prime}\!-\!1)~}{1818816\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{\prime 2}}[(175273\bar{s}^{\prime 3}\!-\!133427\bar{s}^{\prime 2}\!+\!1915\bar{s}^{\prime}\!+\!783)+4\bar{\omega}_{-}(8918\bar{s}^{\prime 2}\!+\!511\bar{s}^{\prime}\!+\!87)c_{\theta}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+(74431\bar{s}^{\prime 3}\!-\!86681\bar{s}^{\prime 2}\!+\!853\bar{s}^{\prime}\!+\!261)c_{2\theta}\hskip 0.85358pt]\,,

(206b)

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{u}^{\mathsf{A}}

\displaystyle=\frac{~M_{n}^{2}(49\bar{s}^{\prime}\!-\!1)~}{1818816\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{\prime 2}}[(26411\bar{s}^{\prime 3}\!-\!9947\bar{s}^{\prime 2}\!+\!8585\bar{s}^{\prime}\!-\!783)+4\bar{\omega}_{-}(1715\bar{s}^{\prime 2}\!+\!364\bar{s}^{\prime}\!-\!87)c_{\theta}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt-(74431\bar{s}^{\prime 3}\!-\!86681\bar{s}^{\prime 2}\!+\!853\bar{s}^{\prime}\!+\!261)c_{2\theta}\hskip 0.85358pt]\,,

(206c)

where we have defined the notations

\,\bar{s}\!=\!s/M_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.85358pt
,

\,\bar{s}^{\prime}\!=\!\bar{s}/49\hskip 0.85358pt
, and

\hskip 0.85358pt{\omega}_{\pm}\hskip-0.85358pt\!=\!\hskip-0.85358pt\sqrt{49\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{\prime\hskip 0.85358pt2}\!\pm 58\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{\prime}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt9\,}\hskip 0.85358pt
. Under high energy expansion, we derive the following LO
kinematic numerators:

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s}^{0,\mathsf{A}}

\displaystyle=\frac{~3\bar{s}^{\prime}M_{n}^{2}~}{16}(35\hskip-0.85358pt+43\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta})\hskip 0.85358pt,

(207a)

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{t}^{0,\mathsf{A}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{~\bar{s}^{\prime}M_{n}^{2}~}{64}(451\hskip-0.85358pt+468\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\!+145\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta})\hskip 0.85358pt,

(207b)

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{u}^{0,\mathsf{A}}

\displaystyle=\frac{~\bar{s}^{\prime}M_{n}^{2}~}{64}(31\hskip-0.85358pt-48\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\!+145\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta})\hskip 0.85358pt.

(207c)

\blacklozenge
Inelastic KK Scattering Amplitudes of

\,\{n,n,m,m
}:

For the inelastic scattering channel
\{n,n,m,m\}
,
there are two types of independent combinations of KK indices,

\mathsf{A}\!=\!\{\pm n,\mp n,\mp m,\pm m\}

and

\mathsf{B}\!=\!\{\pm n,\mp n,\pm m,\mp m\}
. For the type-A scattering,
we compute the kinematic numerators of the KK scattering amplitude

\mathcal{T}\big[\hskip-0.85358ptA^{a\hskip 0.85358pt\pm n}_{L}\hskip-0.85358ptA^{b\hskip 0.85358pt\mp n}_{L}\hskip-0.85358ptA^{c\hskip 0.85358pt\mp m}_{L}\hskip-1.42262ptA^{d\hskip 0.85358pt\pm m}_{L}\big]

as follows:

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07164pt\mathcal{N}_{s}^{\mathsf{A}}

\displaystyle=\,-\frac{~M_{n}^{2}~}{r^{2}}\big[r\hskip 0.85358pt(\bar{s}^{2}\!-\!2\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358ptr_{+}^{2}\!+\!4\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2})\!+\!2\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{q}\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{q}^{\prime}(\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358ptr_{+}^{2}\!+\!2\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2})\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\hskip 0.85358pt\big]\hskip 0.85358pt,

(208a)

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07164pt\mathcal{N}_{t}^{\mathsf{A}}

\displaystyle=\,\frac{~M_{n}^{2}~}{\,8\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2}\,}\big\{\bar{s}^{2}(r_{+}^{2}\!+\!4\hskip 0.85358ptr)\!-\!4\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358pt[r_{+}^{2}\hskip 0.85358ptr\hskip 0.85358pt(r\!+\!2)\!+\!1]\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt16\hskip 0.85358ptr^{3}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt8\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{q}\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{q}^{\prime}[\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358pt(r\!+\!1)^{2}\!+\!2\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2}\hskip 0.85358pt]\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}

\displaystyle\hskip 19.91692pt\!+\!\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358ptr_{+}^{2}(\bar{s}\!-\!4\hskip 0.85358ptr_{+}^{2})\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta}\big\}\hskip 0.85358pt,

(208b)

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07164pt\mathcal{N}_{u}^{\mathsf{A}}

\displaystyle=\,-\frac{~M_{n}^{2}~}{\,8\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2}\,}\big\{\bar{s}^{2}(r_{+}^{2}\!-\!4\hskip 0.85358ptr)\!-\hskip-0.85358pt4\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358pt[\hskip 0.85358ptr_{+}^{2}\hskip 0.85358ptr\hskip 0.85358pt(r\!-\!2)\!+\!1]\!-\!16\hskip 0.85358ptr^{3}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt8\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{q}\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{q}^{\prime}[\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358pt(r\!-\!1)^{2}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2}\hskip 0.85358pt]\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}

\displaystyle\hskip 19.91692pt\!+\!\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358ptr_{+}^{2}(\bar{s}\!-\!4\hskip 0.85358ptr_{+}^{2})\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta}\big\}\hskip 0.85358pt,

(208c)

where the mass ratios
(r,\,r_{+})

and the 3-momenta
(q,\,q^{\prime})
are given by

\displaystyle r=\hskip-0.85358ptM_{m}/M_{n}\hskip 0.85358pt,\hskip 11.38109ptr_{+}^{2}=1+r^{2},\hskip 11.38109ptq\!=\hskip-0.85358pt\sqrt{E^{2}\!-\!M_{n}^{2}\,}\,,\hskip 11.38109ptq^{\prime}\!=\hskip-0.85358pt\sqrt{E^{2}\!-\!M_{m}^{2}\,}\,,

\displaystyle\bar{q}^{2}\!=q^{2}/M_{n}^{2}=\bar{s}/4\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt1\hskip 0.85358pt,\hskip 8.53581pt\bar{q}^{\prime 2}\!=q^{\prime 2}\hskip-0.85358pt/M_{n}^{2}=\bar{s}/4\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358ptr^{2},\hskip 8.53581pt\bar{q}^{2}\bar{q}^{\prime 2}\hskip-0.85358pt=\hskip-0.85358pt(\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt4)(\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt4\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2})/16\hskip 0.85358pt.~~~\hskip 8.53581pt

Then, making the high energy expansion, we derive the following LO
kinematic numerators:

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s}^{0,\mathsf{A}}

\displaystyle=\frac{~\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358ptM_{n}^{2}~}{r^{2}}\big[2\hskip 0.85358ptr\hskip 0.85358ptr_{+}^{2}+(1+r^{2}r_{+}^{2})\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\big]\hskip 0.85358pt,

(210a)

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{t}^{0,\mathsf{A}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{~\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358ptM_{n}^{2}~}{4\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2}}\big[(1\!+\hskip-0.85358pt4\hskip 0.85358ptr\!+\hskip-0.85358pt8\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt4\hskip 0.85358ptr^{3}\!+\hskip-0.85358ptr^{4})\!+2\hskip 0.85358pt(1\!+\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358ptr\!+\hskip-0.85358ptr^{2}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358ptr^{3}\!+\hskip-0.85358ptr^{4})\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\!+\hskip-0.85358ptr_{+}^{4}c_{2\theta}\big]\hskip 0.85358pt,

(210b)

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{u}^{0,\mathsf{A}}

\displaystyle=\frac{~\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358ptM_{n}^{2}~}{4\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2}}\big[(1-4r+8r^{2}-4r^{3}+r^{4})-2(1-2r+r^{2}-2r^{3}+r^{4})c_{\theta}+r_{+}^{4}c_{2\theta}\big]\hskip 0.85358pt.

(210c)

For the type-B scattering,
we compute the kinematic numerators of the KK scattering amplitude

\mathcal{T}\big[\hskip-0.85358ptA^{a\hskip 0.85358pt\pm n}_{L}\hskip-0.85358ptA^{b\hskip 0.85358pt\mp n}_{L}\hskip-0.85358ptA^{c\hskip 0.85358pt\pm m}_{L}\hskip-1.42262ptA^{d\hskip 0.85358pt\mp m}_{L}\big]

as follows:

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07164pt\mathcal{N}_{s}^{\mathsf{B}}\,

\displaystyle=\,\frac{~M_{n}^{2}~}{r^{2}}\big[\hskip 0.85358ptr(\bar{s}^{2}\!-\!2\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358ptr_{+}^{2}\!+\!4\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2})\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{q}\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{q}^{\prime}(\bar{s}r_{+}^{2}\!+\!2\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2})\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\big]\hskip 0.85358pt,

(211a)

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07164pt\mathcal{N}_{t}^{\mathsf{B}}\,

\displaystyle=\,\frac{~M_{n}^{2}~}{8\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2}}\big\{\bar{s}^{2}(r_{+}^{2}\!-\!4\hskip 0.85358ptr)\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt4\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358pt\big[r_{+}^{2}\hskip 0.85358ptr\hskip 0.85358pt(r\!-\!2)\!+\!1\big]\!-\!16r^{3}\!+\!8\bar{q}\bar{q}^{\prime}\big[\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358pt(r\!-\!1)^{2}\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2}\hskip 0.85358pt\big]c_{\theta}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.07164pt+\hskip-0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358ptr_{+}^{2}(\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt4\hskip 0.85358ptr_{+}^{2})\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta}\big\}\hskip 0.85358pt,

(211b)

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07164pt\mathcal{N}_{u}^{\mathsf{B}}\,

\displaystyle=\,-\frac{~M_{n}^{2}~}{8\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2}}\big\{\bar{s}^{2}(r_{+}^{2}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt4\hskip 0.85358ptr)\!-\hskip-0.85358pt4\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\big[r_{+}^{2}\hskip 0.85358ptr\hskip 0.85358pt(r\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt2)\hskip-0.85358pt+\!1\big]\hskip-0.85358pt+\!16\hskip 0.85358ptr^{3}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt8\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{q}\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{q}^{\prime}\big[\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358pt(r\!+\!1)^{2}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2}\hskip 0.85358pt]\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.07164pt\!+\!\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358ptr_{+}^{2}\hskip 0.85358pt(\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt4\hskip 0.85358ptr_{+}^{2})\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta}\big\}\hskip 0.85358pt.

(211c)

Then, under high energy expansion, we derive the following LO
kinematic numerators:

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s}^{0,\mathsf{B}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{~\bar{s}M_{n}^{2}~}{r^{2}}\big[2\hskip 0.85358ptr\hskip 0.85358ptr_{+}^{2}\hskip-0.85358pt-(1\hskip-0.85358pt+r^{2}r_{+}^{2})\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\big],

(212a)

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{t}^{0,\mathsf{B}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{~\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358ptM_{n}^{2}~}{4\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2}}\big[(1\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt4\hskip 0.85358ptr\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt8\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2}\!-4\hskip 0.85358ptr^{3}\!+r^{4})\!+2\hskip 0.85358pt(1\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358ptr\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358ptr^{2}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358ptr^{3}\!+\hskip-0.85358ptr^{4})\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\!+r_{+}^{4}c_{2\theta}\big],

(212b)

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{u}^{0,\mathsf{B}}

\displaystyle=\frac{~\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358ptM_{n}^{2}~}{4\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2}}\big[(1\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt4\hskip 0.85358ptr\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt8\hskip 0.85358ptr^{2}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt4\hskip 0.85358ptr^{3}\!+\hskip-0.85358ptr^{4})\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358pt(1\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358ptr\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358ptr^{2}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358ptr^{3}\!+\hskip-0.85358ptr^{4})\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\!+\hskip-0.85358ptr_{+}^{4}c_{2\theta}\big].

(212c)

\blacklozenge
Elastic KK Scattering Amplitudes of

\,\{n,n,n,n
}:

For the elastic scattering channel
\{n,n,n,n\}
,
there are three types of independent combinations of KK indices,

\mathsf{A}\!=\!\{\pm n,\pm n,\mp n,\mp n\}
,

\mathsf{B}\!=\!\{\pm n,\mp n,\mp n,\pm n\}
, and

\mathsf{C}\!=\!\{\pm n,\mp n,\pm n,\mp n\}
. For the type-A scattering,
we compute the kinematic numerators of the
elastic KK scattering amplitude

\mathcal{T}\big[\hskip-0.85358ptA^{a\hskip 0.85358pt\pm n}_{L}A^{b\hskip 0.85358pt\pm n}_{L}A^{c\hskip 0.85358pt\mp m}_{L}\hskip-1.42262ptA^{d\hskip 0.85358pt\mp m}_{L}\big]

as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s}^{\mathsf{A}}\,

\displaystyle=\,-(2\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{2}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt7\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt4)\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\hskip 0.85358ptM_{n}^{2}\,,

(213a)

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{t}^{\mathsf{A}}\,

\displaystyle=\,\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{\,2\,}$}(\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt4)\big[\hskip 0.85358pt(\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt3)\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt(2\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt+\!1)\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta}\hskip 0.85358pt\big]M_{n}^{2}\,,

(213b)

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{u}^{\mathsf{A}}\,

\displaystyle=\,-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{\,2\,}$}(\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt4)\big[\hskip 0.85358pt(\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt3)\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt(2\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt+\!1)\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta}\hskip 0.85358pt\big]M_{n}^{2}\,.

(213c)

Then, making the high energy expansion, we derive the following
expressions for the LO kinematic numerators of type-A:

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s}^{0,\mathsf{A}}=-s\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\hskip 0.85358pt,\hskip 20.00003pt\mathcal{N}_{t}^{0,\mathsf{A}}=-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{\,2\,}$}s\hskip 0.85358pt(3-c_{\theta})\hskip 0.85358pt,\hskip 20.00003pt\mathcal{N}_{u}^{0,\mathsf{A}}=\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{\,2\,}$}s\hskip 0.85358pt(3+c_{\theta})\hskip 0.85358pt.

(214)

Next, for the type-B elastic KK scattering,
we compute the kinematic numerators of the
elastic KK scattering amplitude

\mathcal{T}\big[\hskip-0.85358ptA^{a\hskip 0.85358pt\pm n}_{L}A^{b\hskip 0.85358pt\mp n}_{L}A^{c\hskip 0.85358pt\mp n}_{L}\hskip-1.42262ptA^{d\hskip 0.85358pt\pm n}_{L}\big]

as follows:

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07164pt\mathcal{N}_{s}^{\mathsf{B}}\,

\displaystyle=\,-[(\bar{s}\!-\!2)^{2}\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt(\bar{s}^{2}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt3\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\!-\!4)\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}]M_{n}^{2}\,,

(215a)

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07164pt\mathcal{N}_{t}^{\mathsf{B}}\,

\displaystyle=\,\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{\,4\,}$}\big[(3\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{2}\!-\!14\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt8)\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358pt(2\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{2}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt7\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt4)\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358pt(\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt8)\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta}\hskip 0.85358pt\big]M_{n}^{2}\,,

(215b)

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07164pt\mathcal{N}_{u}^{\mathsf{B}}\,

\displaystyle=\,\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{\,4\,}$}\big[(\bar{s}^{2}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt8)\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358pt(\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt4)\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358pt(\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt8)\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta}\hskip 0.85358pt\big]M_{n}^{2}\,.

(215c)

Then, under high energy expansion, we derive the following formulas
for the LO kinematic numerators of type-B:

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s}^{0,\mathsf{B}}\!=s\hskip 0.85358pt(4+\hskip-0.85358pt3\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta})\hskip 0.85358pt,\hskip 10.00002pt\mathcal{N}_{t}^{0,\mathsf{B}}\!=-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{\,2\,}$}\hskip 0.85358pts\hskip 0.85358pt(9\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt7\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta})\hskip 0.85358pt,\hskip 10.00002pt\mathcal{N}_{u}^{0,\mathsf{B}}\!=\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{\,2\,}$}s(1\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358ptc_{\theta}\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta})\hskip 0.85358pt.

(216)

Finally, for the type-C elastic KK scattering,
we compute the kinematic numerators of the
elastic KK scattering amplitude

\mathcal{T}\big[\hskip-0.85358ptA^{a\hskip 0.85358pt\pm n}_{L}A^{b\hskip 0.85358pt\mp n}_{L}A^{c\hskip 0.85358pt\pm n}_{L}\hskip-1.42262ptA^{d\hskip 0.85358pt\mp n}_{L}\big]

as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s}^{\mathsf{C}}

\displaystyle=\,\big[(\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt2)^{2}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt(\bar{s}^{2}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt3\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt4)\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\hskip 0.85358pt\big]M_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.85358pt,

(217a)

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{t}^{\mathsf{C}}

\displaystyle=\,-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{\,4\,}$}\big[(\bar{s}^{2}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt8)\!-\!2\hskip 0.85358pt(\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt4)\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358pt(\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt8)\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta}\hskip 0.85358pt\big]M_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.85358pt,

(217b)

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{u}^{\mathsf{C}}

\displaystyle=\,-\frac{{1}}{4}[(3\bar{s}^{2}\!-\!14\bar{s}\!+\!8)\!-\!2(2\bar{s}^{2}\!-\!7\bar{s}\!-\!4)c_{\theta}\!+\!\bar{s}(\bar{s}\!-\!8)c_{2\theta}]M_{n}^{2}\,.

(217c)

Then, making the high energy expansion, we derive the following
expressions for the LO kinematic numerators of type-C:

\mathcal{N}_{s}^{0,\mathsf{C}}\!=-s\hskip 0.85358pt(4\!-\hskip-0.85358pt3\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta})\hskip 0.85358pt,\hskip 10.00002pt\mathcal{N}_{t}^{0,\mathsf{C}}\!=-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{\,2\,}$}s\hskip 0.85358pt(1\!-\hskip-0.85358ptc_{\theta}\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta})\hskip 0.85358pt,\hskip 10.00002pt\mathcal{N}_{u}^{0,\mathsf{C}}\!=\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{\,2\,}$}s\hskip 0.85358pt(9\!-\hskip-0.85358pt7\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt2\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta})\hskip 0.85358pt.

(218)

Appendix C
Full Scattering Amplitudes of KK Gravitons by Double-Copy

In this Appendix, we present the full massive scattering amplitudes
of KK gravitons for various four-particle elastic and inelastic
scattering processes at the tree level. The scattering amplitudes for these processes have been studied
in Sec.
3.2
by using the leading-order longitudinal
polarization tensor (
53
)
for each external KK graviton state. In the following, we derive the full scattering amplitudes
of four KK gravitons by using the exact
polarization tensors in Eq.(
25
).

\blacklozenge
Inelastic KK Scattering Amplitudes of

\,\{0,0,n,n
}:

As the first eaxmple, we present the four-point full scattering amplitude
of zero-mode and KK gauge bosons, by using the exact KK graviton polarization tensors (
25
). The amplitudes under
S^{1}
and
S^{1}\!/\mathbb{Z}_{2}
compactification
are equal and take the following form:

\mathcal{M}\!\left[h^{0}_{\pm 2}h^{0}_{\mp 2}h^{\pm n}_{L}h^{\mp n}_{L}\right]=\mathcal{M}\!\left[h^{0}_{\pm 2}h^{0}_{\mp 2}h^{n}_{L}h^{n}_{L}\right]=\frac{~\kappa^{2}\hskip-0.85358ptM_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.85358pt(\bar{s}^{2}\!+\!16\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt+\!16)\hskip 0.85358pts^{4}_{\theta}~}{{8}\hskip 0.85358pt[(\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt4)\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt(\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt4)c_{2\theta}]}\hskip 0.85358pt,

(219)

where we have defined

\,\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt=\hskip-0.85358pts/M_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.85358pt
.
Then, making the high energy expansion, we derive its LO and NLO
amplitudes as follows:

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}_{0}\big[h_{\pm 2}^{0}h_{\mp 2}^{0}\bar{h}_{L}^{\mp n}\bar{h}_{L}^{\pm n}\big]\hskip 0.85358pt

\displaystyle=\hskip 0.85358pt\frac{\,\kappa^{2}}{~{16}~}s\hskip 0.85358pts_{\theta}^{2}\,,

(220a)

\displaystyle{\delta\mathcal{M}}\big[h_{\pm 2}^{0}h_{\mp 2}^{0}\bar{h}_{L}^{\mp n}\bar{h}_{L}^{\pm n}\big]\hskip 0.85358pt

\displaystyle=\hskip 0.85358pt\frac{~\kappa^{2}M_{n}^{2}\,}{{8}}(3-5\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta})\,.

(220b)

S^{1}
compactification, we derive the full elastic KK graviton
scattering amplitudes as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}\big[h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\mp n}h_{L}^{\mp n}\big]

\displaystyle=\frac{\,\kappa^{2}M_{n}^{2}\,}{{64}}(\bar{s}-4)(7+c_{2\theta})^{2}\hskip-0.85358pt\csc^{2}\hskip-1.42262pt\theta\,,

(230a)

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}\big[h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\mp n}h_{L}^{\mp n}h_{L}^{\pm n}\big]

\displaystyle=\frac{~\kappa^{2}M_{n}^{2}(X_{0}\!+\!X_{1}c_{\theta}\!+\!X_{2}c_{2\theta}\!+\!X_{3}c_{3\theta}\!+\!X_{4}c_{4\theta})\csc^{2}\!\frac{\theta}{2}~}{{256}\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358pt(\bar{s}-4)[(\bar{s}+4)\!+\!(\bar{s}-4)c_{\theta}]}\,,

(230b)

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}\big[h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\mp n}h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\mp n}\big]

\displaystyle=\frac{~\kappa^{2}M_{n}^{2}(X_{0}\!-\!X_{1}c_{\theta}\!+\!X_{2}c_{2\theta}\!-\!X_{3}c_{3\theta}\!+\!X_{4}c_{4\theta})\sec^{2}\!\frac{\theta}{2}~}{{256}\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358pt(\bar{s}-4)[(\bar{s}+4)\!-\!(\bar{s}-4)c_{\theta}]}\,,

(230c)

where

\displaystyle X_{0}

\displaystyle=99\bar{s}^{4}-672\bar{s}^{3}+3168\bar{s}^{2}-7680\bar{s}+8960\,,

(231)

\displaystyle X_{1}

\displaystyle=128(3\bar{s}^{3}-24\bar{s}^{2}+76\bar{s}-112)\,,

\displaystyle X_{2}

\displaystyle=4(7\bar{s}^{4}+32\bar{s}^{3}-96\bar{s}^{2}-256\bar{s}+1792)\,,

\displaystyle X_{3}

\displaystyle=128\bar{s}^{3}-1536\bar{s}-2048\,,

\displaystyle X_{4}

\displaystyle=\bar{s}^{4}+32\bar{s}^{3}+288\bar{s}^{2}+512\bar{s}+256\,.

Under the high energy expansion, we derive the LO and NLO
elastic KK graviton scattering amplitudes as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{0}\big[h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\mp n}h_{L}^{\mp n}\big]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{M}_{0}\big[h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\mp n}h_{L}^{\mp n}h_{L}^{\pm n}\big]=\mathcal{M}_{0}\big[h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\mp n}h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\mp n}\big]\hskip 34.1433pt

\displaystyle=\frac{~\kappa^{2}\hskip 0.85358pts~}{\,64\,}\hskip 0.85358pt(7\hskip-1.42262pt+\hskip-0.85358ptc_{2\theta})^{2}\hskip-0.85358pt\csc^{2}\!\theta\,,

(232a)

\displaystyle\delta\mathcal{M}\big[h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\mp n}h_{L}^{\mp n}\big]

\displaystyle=-\frac{~\kappa^{2}\hskip 0.85358ptM_{n}^{2}~}{\,16\,}\hskip 0.85358pt(7+\hskip-0.85358ptc_{2\theta})^{2}\!\csc^{2}\!\theta\,,

(232b)

\displaystyle\delta\mathcal{M}\big[h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\mp n}h_{L}^{\mp n}h_{L}^{\pm n}\big]

\displaystyle=\frac{~\kappa^{2}M_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.85358pt(-70+\!196\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}+\hskip-0.85358pt443\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta}\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt282\hskip 0.85358ptc_{3\theta}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt134\hskip 0.85358ptc_{4\theta}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt34\hskip 0.85358ptc_{5\theta}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt5\hskip 0.85358ptc_{6\theta})}{8\hskip 0.85358pt(5\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt4\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt4\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta}\!-4\hskip 0.85358ptc_{3\theta}\!-\hskip-0.85358ptc_{4\theta})}\hskip 0.85358pt,

(232c)

\displaystyle\delta\mathcal{M}\big[h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\mp n}h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\mp n}\big]

\displaystyle=\frac{~\kappa^{2}M_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.85358pt(-70\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt196\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\!+443\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt282\hskip 0.85358ptc_{3\theta}\!+\!134\hskip 0.85358ptc_{4\theta}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt34\hskip 0.85358ptc_{5\theta}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt5\hskip 0.85358ptc_{6\theta})~}{8\hskip 0.85358pt(5\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt4\hskip 0.85358ptc_{\theta}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt4\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt4\hskip 0.85358ptc_{3\theta}\!-\hskip-0.85358ptc_{4\theta})}\hskip 0.85358pt.

(232d)

Next, we consider the
S^{1}\!/\mathbb{Z}_{2}
compactification and
compute the elastic scattering amplitude of
longitudinal KK gravitons as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}\big[h^{n}_{L}\hskip 0.85358pth^{n}_{L}\hskip 0.85358pth^{n}_{L}\hskip 0.85358pth^{n}_{L}\big]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left\{\mathcal{M}\big[h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\mp n}h_{L}^{\mp n}\big]\!+\!\mathcal{M}\big[h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\mp n}h_{L}^{\mp n}h_{L}^{\pm n}\big]\!+\!\mathcal{M}\big[h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\mp n}h_{L}^{\pm n}h_{L}^{\mp n}\big]\right\}

\displaystyle=-\frac{~\kappa^{2}M_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.85358pt(X_{0}\hskip-0.85358pt+\!X_{2}c_{2\theta}\hskip-0.85358pt+\!X_{4}c_{4\theta}\hskip-0.85358pt+\!X_{6}c_{6\theta})\hskip-0.85358pt\csc^{2}\!\theta~}{~512\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip 0.85358pt(\bar{s}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt4)[\hskip 0.85358pt(\bar{s}^{2}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt24\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt+\!16)\!-\hskip-0.85358pt(\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt4)^{2}c_{2\theta}]~}\,,

(233)

where the coefficients
\{X_{j}\}

are polynomial functions given by

\displaystyle X_{0}

\displaystyle=-2\hskip 0.85358pt(255\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{5}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt2824\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{4}\!-\!19936\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{3}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt39936\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{2}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt256\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt+\!14336)\hskip 0.85358pt,

(234)

\displaystyle X_{2}

\displaystyle=429\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{5}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt10152\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{4}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt30816\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{3}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt27136\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{2}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt49920\hskip-0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt+\hskip-0.85358pt34816\hskip 0.85358pt,

\displaystyle X_{4}

\displaystyle=2\hskip 0.85358pt(39\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{5}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt312\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{4}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt2784\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{3}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt11264\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{2}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt26368\hskip-0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\!2048)\hskip 0.85358pt,

\displaystyle X_{6}

\displaystyle=3\bar{s}^{5}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt40\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{4}\!+\hskip-0.85358pt416\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{3}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt1536\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}^{2}\!-\hskip-0.85358pt3328\hskip 0.85358pt\bar{s}\hskip-0.85358pt-\hskip-0.85358pt2048\hskip 0.85358pt.

Then, making the high energy expansion,
we derive the following LO and NLO elastic
scattering amplitudes of KK gravitons:

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{0}\big[h_{L}^{n}h_{L}^{n}h_{L}^{n}h_{L}^{n}\big]=\frac{~3\hskip 0.85358pt\kappa^{2}\,}{\,128\,}\hskip 0.85358pts\hskip 0.85358pt(7\hskip-0.85358pt+c_{2\theta})^{2}\!\csc^{2}\!\theta\,,

(235a)

\displaystyle\delta\mathcal{M}\big[h_{L}^{n}h_{L}^{n}h_{L}^{n}h_{L}^{n}\big]=-\frac{\,\kappa^{2}M_{n}^{2}~}{256}(1810+93\hskip 0.85358ptc_{2\theta}\hskip-1.42262pt+\hskip-0.85358pt126\hskip 0.85358ptc_{4\theta}\hskip-1.42262pt+\hskip-0.85358pt19\hskip 0.85358ptc_{6\theta})\hskip-0.85358pt\csc^{4}\!\theta\hskip 0.85358pt.

(235b)
